Expected: sheet renders normally with card backgrounds and ink ripples on tiles; no error screen (debug builds previously crashed with a ListTile paint assertion). | ⬜ | epic:PON-E-flutter-344-test-recovery · story:PON-S-reader-settings-material · sprint:2026-08-05 |

| PON-T-817 | Time away from the reader is not reading time | On an Android phone open Journey › Statistics and note today’s Qur’an Reading time. Open the Qur’an reader, read for about 30 seconds, press Home and wait two minutes, come back, read about 30 seconds more, close the reader and return to Statistics. Expected: today’s time grows by about one minute, not three, and sessions tracked by one. Repeat on the web build, switching to another browser tab for the two minutes instead of pressing Home: the same. On iPhone it still holds. With Keep playing in the background off, a recitation playing in the reader pauses when you press Home on Android (as on iPhone); on the web a hidden tab keeps reciting. | ⬜ | epic:PON-E-quran-playback-integrity · story:PON-S-reader-away-not-reading · sprint:2026-09-27 |
